# Paragon PR7-S: notes for Claude

**Paragon PR7-S** is the Paragon PR7 family's **studio**: a standalone DAW (AGPLv3, JUCE, a program, not a plugin) with a workflow **inspired by Cubase** and the PR7 family's look. The owner chose the name (S for studio).

| Member | Repo |
|---|---|
| PR7-G (guitar; the studio's guitar effects since 0.204.0, read-only reference at ARYEZ/Paragon-PR7-G) | `ARYEZ/Paragon-X1` (now Paragon-PR7-G) |
| PR7-B (bass) | `ARYEZ/Paragon-PR7-B` |
| PR7-V (vocals) | `ARYEZ/Paragon-PR7-V` |
| PR7-C (channel strip) | `ARYEZ/Paragon-PR7-C` |
| PR7-K (synth: the studio's synth since 0.9.11) | `ARYEZ/Paragon-PR7-K` |
| PR7-D (drums: the studio's drums since 0.107.0, the Backline) | `ARYEZ/Paragon-PR7-D` |

## The plan (agreed with the owner; one release each)

1. **0.1.0, done: MIDI and instruments** (the owner's choice to start). Instrument tracks (the built-in Paragon Synth or VST3 instruments), MIDI recording from a keyboard or the on-screen one, the arrangement, transport, click, cycle, songs saved as `.pr7s`, undo.
2. **0.2.0, done: the piano roll (key editor):** draw, move, copy, resize, delete notes; velocity lane; quantize; in the lower zone, following the selected part.
3. **0.3.0, done: the mixer:** channel strips, inserts (VST3 effects, including our PR7 plugins; 16 slots since 0.5.1), master bus with its own inserts; the on-screen keyboard removed (the owner asked: the lower zone needs the room).
4. **0.4.0, done: audio tracks:** record through the interface (mono or stereo input, latency-compensated), clips with waveform, trim start/end, fades, monitoring through inserts; ASIO on Windows.
5. **0.5.0, done: export** (a mixdown to WAV; the owner agreed to split automation off). **0.5.1:** fixes from checking 0.1–0.5 against the Cubase 15 manual, and family colours only.
6. **0.6.0, done: automation** (Touch writing; lanes, R/W, drawing and editing; the master's row).
7. **0.7.0, done: sends, FX and group channels** (the owner's choice after automation). **0.7.1:** MIDI file import (the owner asked). **0.7.2:** zoom (H in, G out, Shift+F), Auto-Scroll (F), scroll bars, the lower zone resizable, the mixer's rack hidden (the owner asked; the owner remembered G/H the other way round, the manual has H in). **0.7.3:** vertical zoom (Shift+H/G, Ctrl+Shift+wheel), Auto-Scroll's menu (Page Scroll / Stationary Cursor, Suspend Auto-Scroll when Editing).
8. **0.8.0, done: editing tools** (the owner's choice): the arrangement's TOOLS row and keys 1, 3–9 (SELECT, SPLIT, GLUE, ERASE, ZOOM, MUTE, DRAW, PLAY), F9/F10, Alt+X, muted parts. **0.8.1:** Range Selection (2): ranges across tracks, Split/Crop/Delete/Delete Time/Cut/Cut Time/Copy/Paste/Paste Time/Insert Silence/Duplicate, move and Alt-copy by drag. Not yet: multiple selection, automation in ranges. **0.8.2:** locators (Ctrl/Alt/Ctrl+Alt-click, handles, P / Alt+P, number pad 1/2), tracks (drag to reorder, multiple selection, Up/Down, duplicate, remove selected/empty, double-click to add), SPAN ALL SCREENS and the window's place kept (the owner has three monitors). **0.8.3:** audio file import (IMPORT, drag and drop), the CPU meter, the wide layout (the owner's spanned screenshot: the position screen had stretched across all three). **0.8.4:** Render in Place, tool icons and pointers, lit keys black, AUTO-SCROLL and a CPU screen on the transport, + ADD TRACK at the top, Ctrl/Alt in the whole ruler, the spanned view's mixer, family colours for drop-downs and menus (the owner's list). **0.8.5:** the buffer size (the owner's AXE I/O offered only a small one): ASIO first, SETTINGS explains, RELOAD DRIVER. **0.8.6:** plug-ins rendered silence (offline mode without a new set-up).
9. **0.9.0, done: the Inspector** (the owner's screenshot of Cubase's; sections chosen by the owner): Basics, Routing (MIDI IN, CHANNEL, input, OUT), Inserts, Sends, Fader. Track presets and Freeze done in 0.9.8. EQ later (the owner's choice). Not wanted: Track Versions, MIDI Inserts. **0.9.1:** disable / hide tracks, the left zone's VISIBILITY tab, render's Disable / Hide source. **0.9.2:** the lag (whole-view redraws 30 times a second) fixed, several parts selected (rectangle, Shift-click, Ctrl+A; move, copy, paste, duplicate, delete), the Inspector's width and 5 insert slots, track names large without the instrument. **0.9.3:** the key editor at 200% (SIZE), pencil and scissors pointers, SPLIT (3), 1/1 and 1/2 grids. **0.9.4:** random velocities (LOW/MID/HIGH, Alt+B/N/M, ranges kept). **0.9.5:** importing several files (MIDI chooser takes several; audio: one track or a track each). **0.9.6:** Quick Controls (8 per track in the Inspector). **0.9.7:** IMPORT's choices all in its menu (a chooser opened from a question box's answer closed with it on Windows). **0.9.8:** Freeze (tail sizes, keep/delete the file on unfreeze) and track presets (save, load, a new track from one). **0.9.9:** the click stick (hasEditor asked of Kontakt after every change; the screen's engine reads through the audio lock), renders switching only the sounding plugins, hidden solos, rows without M S REC R W, typed fader/pan values. **0.9.10:** question boxes in the family's look, POSITION and TIME screens, F1/F2/F3 (Alt: docked/full screen), the key editor renamed MIDI editor, SNAP (J) and the quantize value on the header, the MIDI editor's panel one column half as wide, velocity handles. **0.9.11:** PR7-K the studio's synth (shipped beside the app), the cycle bar's gradient, SPLIT's ghost lines, zone windows across all screens, SAVE in the background, quieter parts, Add Track in the right-click menus. **0.9.12:** plug-in windows float above the studio (and pass its keys), the master's row at the top with its own Inspector, only the gradient's three colours (tracks, notes by velocity), the play bar pearl white with a pink glow, the track menu A to Z. **0.9.13:** DAWproject import (songs from Cubase: File > Export > DAWproject). **0.9.14:** the mixer's window shows inserts and sends together (docked: the choice, faders only at first), MIDI import asks to match the song to the file's tempo, the session log (crashes and freezes in a readable file).
10. **0.10.0, done: the tempo track** (the owner asked: as Cubase's). Not yet: the Tempo Track Editor window (Ctrl+T), its Inspector event list, Process Tempo, tempo recording/tap/detection, the smart controls, a signature track, musical-mode audio.
11. **0.11.0, done: key commands and macros** (the owner asked, for an Elgato Stream Deck: its Hotkey buttons press the keys given here). Not yet: the MIDI editor's own keys in KEYS. **0.11.1 (released inside 0.12.0: its macOS test asked for a window taller than CI's screen):** the progress box (Render in Place, Freeze, opening a song's plug-ins; every progress bar a meter), the select box scrolls past the edges, SAVE lights up when saved, the mixer window's racks twice the size. The owner saw a mock-up of parts drawn as mixer channels and kept today's look.
12. **0.12.0, done: the Hub** (the owner's screenshot of the other DAW's: a start window opening on the projects folder). **0.12.1:** Render in Place and Freeze render on a thread of their own (they hung the studio for the owner: rendered on the message thread, an offline instrument waiting for its loaders, which waited for the message thread). 
13. **0.13.0, done: the Tempo Track Editor** (the owner asked: as Cubase's): its window (Ctrl+T), toolbar, info line, ruler, signature display, tempo scale, curve, the events listed (our place for the manual's Inspector list), Process Tempo. Not yet: signature changes, tap/recording/detection, Process Bars, smart controls. **0.13.1:** a review of the whole code (four reviewers, every finding checked; `tests/ReviewTests.cpp`). 
14. **0.14.0, done: chord pads** (the owner's choice; the manual's "Using Chord Pads"): the zone (F4), pads, chord editor, modifiers, adaptive voicing, root key, scale and own presets, overlaps, display, trigger keys, a pad to a part. **0.15.0:** the owner's priority is accurate chords (mostly plain major and minor): keys spelled (all 30), a key's chords onto the pads (minor: both v and V), voicings by name, the circle of fifths with scored suggestions (before the chord track: the owner's choice), the pads on the middle screen. **0.16.0:** the chord track (the owner chose the suggested first release). **0.16.1:** a crash fix (the owner's log: `suggestNext` searched from `key.scale().begin()` to another call's `.end()`, two different lists; **never take begin and end from two calls of a function that returns a container**), secondary dominants named by the key's own chord, session log notes one per line. **0.17.0:** the pads on three screens (the keys left, the circle right, the owner asked) and link groups (the owner asked: as the manual's Linking Channels). **0.17.1:** the Inspector's RENDER (Render in Place as last time) in FREEZE's place, DISABLE/ENABLE TRACK always shown, the key's ten most used chords beside the suggestions. **0.17.2:** popular progressions in their place (what the owner meant). **0.17.3:** Change Instrument fixed (instrument ids fell into the Output check), the colour menu (only the family's tints), note ticks on the ruler (the owner asked). **0.17.4:** the playhead glides into place while stopped and the ruler shows where a click lands (the owner asked: it felt static). **0.17.5:** the MIDI editor's playhead glides too. **0.18.0:** bass and melody ideas for the chord track's chords (the owner asked, after asking about another chord tool's features; our own rules and names). **0.18.1:** MIDI files dropped on an instrument track land on it, snapped, with a gradient preview while dragging (the owner's report: drops made new tracks). **0.18.2:** the preview stronger (60%), its notes drawn in it, its strength a Hub setting (the owner asked). **0.19.0:** IDEAS in tabs (the owner asked): piano chords, guitar rhythm (power chords, chugs, gallop...) and lead (pentatonic, blues, shred runs) for the owner's 7-string drop A guitars (Shreddage Jupiter and Rogue), a TUNING, USE IT onto the selected track. **0.19.1:** the guitar ideas' velocities in Shreddage's layout (the owner's: 1-59 palm mute, tighter lower; 60-119 sustain; 120-127 pinch harmonics and rakes). **0.19.2:** rakes 120-126, pinch harmonics 127 (the owner's split). **0.19.3:** the VELOCITIES panel (the owner sets each articulation's velocity by ear). **0.20.0:** humanize (the owner asked): IDEAS' HUMANIZE (amount, FEEL) and the MIDI editor's HUMANIZE, velocities kept in their layers. **0.20.1:** Humanize a key command (Alt+H: the editor's selected notes, else the arrangement's selected parts) and a HUMANIZE section in the Hub's settings (the key, the amount, the layers). **0.20.2:** IDEAS from the chord pads (the owner looked for it there): an IDEAS key on the pads' toolbar, the PLAYER menu's placeholder now opens IDEAS. **0.21.0:** the MediaBay window (F5; the owner chose the core first: locations scanned, results with search and filters, ratings, the previewer, double-click and drag in). **0.21.1:** tags (the manual's Attribute Inspector and Attribute Filter: category/style and their subs, character pairs, tempo, key, rating, comment, user attributes; kept in MediaBay.xml, not written into the files: our choice). **0.22.0:** the Media rack in the right zone (Ctrl+Alt+R; home tiles, instruments, effects, loops, presets, favorites, file browser, drags into the song). **0.22.1:** the MediaBay window's folder tree (the owner's screenshot: the File Browser with a box per folder: tick, square, empty). **0.22.2:** the MediaBay opens where it was left (the owner asked), tempo/key/tags read from inside files (ACID, Apple loops, the other DAW's iXML; the owner's order: this, then 0.23.0 previews in time with the song (Wait for Project Play, Link to Project Tempo with a time-stretcher), then 0.24.0 plug-in presets). **0.23.0:** previews in time with the song (LINK TEMPO with Signalsmith Stretch, WAIT FOR PLAY locked to the bars, transpose / to key, half / double speed). **0.24.0:** VST presets (.vstpreset) in the MediaBay and the rack: read, tagged, matched to their plug-in, a track with one or onto a track. **0.25.0:** MIDI controller lanes (the owner asked; then, the owner's order: 0.26.0 a drum editor with drum maps built in naming each note's drum, then automation as complete as the manual's (typed values for points, several points selected, the panel's modes...), then takes and comping). **0.26.0:** the drum editor and drum maps (GM Map built in, .drm files, the Drum Map Setup). **0.27.0:** automation editing (typed values, Ctrl/Shift/box selection, the event editor's handles, bent ramps, quick scaling, DRAW/ERASE/LINE on lanes, copy/paste automation). **0.28.0:** the Automation panel (F6: modes, Trim, Fill, Suspend, Show, functions, virgin territory, settings). **0.29.0:** takes and comping, part one (lanes, the clip in front, the record modes, the COMP tool, To Front, lane solo, Clean Up Lanes, Create Tracks from Lanes). **0.30.0:** comping, part two (Ctrl audition, dragging splits and edges, Alt-click splits all, the comp handle, Slip Event, Bounce, Delete Overlaps, Merge MIDI in Loop). Not yet: auto fades/crossfades between comped takes, range double-click glue. **0.31.0:** Render in Place's defaults 32-bit float and the original muted/disabled/hidden (the owner's), whole-track renders narrowed to the range or the cycle, Alt+R renders as last time (Read All lost its key), Q from anywhere to the header's quantize value, INSTRUMENT in the editors' panel. **0.32.0:** hitpoints and audio warping (the first of the owner's four: then 0.33.0 Audio Alignment, designed from the manual's Audio Alignment panel and VocAlign 6 Pro's manual (the owner's link): continuous matching of energy patterns, tightness (max difference), flexibility (how far a stretch may go), max shift, only where both play, sync points as suggestions, protected ranges, several targets; the result as warp markers; then VariAudio in two parts). **0.33.0:** Audio Alignment (the manual's panel: reference, targets, Match Words, Prefer Time Shifting, Alignment Precision; ours after VocAlign: only where both play, flexibility, max shift, sync points (red when unmet), protected ranges; splits at the overlap with 10 ms crossfades; warp markers). Not yet: pitch matching of doubles (after VariAudio), several references. **0.34.0:** VariAudio part one (the manual's VariAudio section: Edit VariAudio, segments, pitch snap modes, Correct Pitch, Straighten Curve, segment volume, the Scale Assistant (editor scale / chord track, suggestions, snap, guides, Quantize Pitches), bypass, audition; our own window opened by double-clicking an audio clip). **0.34.1:** the speed review's safe fixes (the owner asked for a review before part two: four reviewers, audio thread / screen / studio / build; every claim checked): trim read in place, flush-to-zero, undo without plug-in states, caches emptied with the song, the warp cache by size, the track list not on sideways scrolls; **0.34.2:** the engine (events kept unless what they're built from changed: a fader move 8.5 ms -> 0.07 ms per update on 16x2000 notes; clips placed once and mixed in one go in the middle; mix lanes only; unmoving gains in one go; one walk for held notes); **0.34.3:** DAWproject import fixes (the owner's Cubase song: new tracks took imported ids, rack instruments as audio tracks, a second master) and the screen's first part (change versions, send slots, timers while hidden); **0.34.4:** the screen's second part (lanes drawn only in the redrawn strip, ArrangeView's rows once per paint, the transport's screens outside the strip skipped); released inside 0.34.5: its macOS strip test was off by one pixel, CoreGraphics' edges, now `SoftwareImageType`). **0.34.5:** multi-core audio (the owner asked: tracks on all cores, bit-identical output; 2.8x on 4 cores here). **0.35.0:** VariAudio part two (segment editing, tilt/rotate/anchor/straighten range, formants, warp start/end, Extract MIDI, harmony voices, MIDI Input/Step, reference track, colours) and the Inspector's LANES | MON row under RENDER/PRESET (the owner asked; off the track rows). Also: the Mac CI job runs on `macos-15-intel` since 0.33.0's release (GitHub's arm64 Macs had no capacity; the app stays universal). **0.36.0:** Follow Chord Track (the owner asked: Root Notes, Chords, Scales, Chords & Scales; the first-time dialog; Live Input; Shift Chords; Map to Chord Track). **0.36.1:** track names in the track list run up to the meter (the owner asked: the row's right strip was still 180 px from MON/LANES; now 76, `rightWidth`). **0.37.0:** the info line for automation points (the owner's screenshot: relative edits of the selection, Ctrl absolute) and Follow Chord Track's Voicings / Single Voice (our voicing libraries). **0.38.0:** the MIDI editor's info line (the owner asked: as the automation's; pitch typed as a note name moves the selection). **0.39.0:** performances (the owner asked for their chord tool's features, in order: 0.39.0 patterns, 0.40.0 a progression library by genre/mood, 0.41.0 better melodies (density, range, steps vs leaps, motifs), 0.42.0 chord detection from MIDI and audio): 24 patterns in IDEAS' PATTERNS tab and PERFORM on the pads. Not yet: audio (VariAudio) following, the info line for parts and events, recording/step input onto the chord track, chords from audio (not promised); piano/guitar players and Pattern mode; Sections and the remote dialog (MIDI Learn). **0.40.0:** the progression library (157 of ours by genre and mood; PROGRESSIONS on the pads, the chord track's menu). **0.41.0:** better melodies (IDEAS' MELODY tab: six forms, MELODY SETTINGS: density, range, leaps, phrase, repeat, rests, syncopation). **0.42.0:** chord detection from MIDI and audio (the part menu's Detect Chords; the MIDI editor's info line names the selection's chord). That finishes the owner's four chord-tool asks. Then the owner chose, in order: 0.43.0 the channel EQ, 0.44.0 the chord tool round two, 0.45.0 tempo and signature, 0.46.0 the info line for parts. **0.43.0:** the channel EQ (four bands and the Pre section on every channel; the Inspector's EQUALIZERS, the EQ window). **0.44.0:** the chord tool round two (recording chords, Sections, REMOTE with MIDI Learn and modifiers, your own patterns, PERFORM on a track). **0.45.0:** tempo and signature (signature changes on a signature track and in the Tempo Track Editor, TAP / Shift+Space with the six modes, the Tempo Detection panel). **0.45.1:** a click on an automation lane adds the point on the line (the line's value there, or the control's on an empty lane; the owner: they took the mouse's height), Ctrl+Enter on both info lines (`InfoEntry`: JUCE's TextEditor answers only a plain Enter). **0.46.0:** the channel EQ as four bands of the other DAW's Frequency 2 (the owner asked; core + spectrum + Auto Listen, in place of the old bands; the info line for parts moves to 0.47.0). **0.46.1:** a click adds a point between points too (on the line; the owner's wish over the manual's rule), Delete selects the next point (the owner asked). **0.47.0:** the info line for parts and events (FILE/NAME/START/END/LENGTH/OFFSET/FADES/VOLUME/MUTE, MIDI TRANSPOSE/VELOCITY; an event's volume and a part's transpose/velocity new, playback only). **0.48.0:** PR7-G 0.59.0's look on the neon finishes (black keys in neon rings: `Finish::neonRings`). Then the owner chose, in order: 0.49.0 markers, 0.50.0 folder tracks, 0.51.0 export round two (stems, MIDI file export), 0.52.0 auto crossfades for comping. **0.49.0:** markers (the marker track, position and cycle markers, the Markers window, the manual's keys, MIDI file markers). **0.50.0:** folder tracks (holding and nesting tracks, folding, M/S for all, folder blocks, a folder's group channel, DAWproject folders). **0.51.0:** export round two (channels a file each, cycle markers a file each, MIDI file export). **0.52.0:** auto fades and crossfades (the manual's Auto Fades dialog: crossfades at comp cuts on by default, fade in/out, 1-500 ms, Equal Power/Gain, the song's or a track's own). That finishes the owner's four. **0.52.1:** Render in Place's Disable/Hide greyed out under CYCLE (whole tracks stay whole, over the cycle; a warning when parts lie outside) and the session log's freeze report rebuilt (steps under way, every thread without the loader lock, STUCK renders, closing watched; the owner's hangs left logs ending on what had finished), the export pages bigger (`ScaledPage`), plug-in windows on the right-hand screen (`Windows::rightmostScreenArea`). **0.52.2:** plug-in windows remember their place (per plug-in name), PLUGINS in the Hub's look (`PluginsPage`), the process ended after closing (`SessionLog::endProcessNow`: the owner's studio stayed in Task Manager). **0.52.3:** the app's icon (the "7S" monogram; `Logo::drawAppIcon`) and Stream Deck icons in every zip. Then the owner chose, in order: 0.53.0 better guitar ideas, 0.54.0 a DRUMS tab and bass locked to the kick (the guitar tab's STYLE drives them too: the owner agreed; both generated beats from scratch and the MIDI grooves in the owner's scanned library: the owner's choice), 0.55.0 pads that build a whole section onto tracks; and asked (from the PR7-D chat) for parts dragged out of the arrangement as files (0.53.1, before the drums). **0.53.0:** guitar STYLE (rock, thrash, nu-metal, metalcore, shred: the owner's list) and RIFF SETTINGS (length, palm mutes, tightness, busyness, fills). **0.53.1:** parts dragged out of the arrangement as .mid / .wav files (for PR7-D's GROOVES page, the desktop, other programs). **0.54.0:** IDEAS' DRUMS tab (written beats per STYLE, one locked to the riff; the library's grooves) and the bass locked to the kick / following the riff. Then the owner asked for beats generated by rules, then learned from real drummers: their folder of whole-song drum MIDI (one file a song, no section names), sections found (intro, verse, chorus, bridge, breakdown, ending) and checked on a review page, profiles per section. Order: 0.55.0 the rule-based generator, 0.56.0 song analysis and learned profiles, 0.57.0 pads that build a whole section. **0.55.0:** the beat generator (one beat per section, DRUM SETTINGS; the written beats behind WRITTEN BEATS). **0.56.0:** learning a drummer (a folder of whole-song drum MIDI; sections found and named from the drums, put right on LEARN A DRUMMER; a profile per section; DRUMMER on the DRUMS tab plays from it). **0.57.0:** BUILD SECTION (the owner chose it in IDEAS, drums + bass + guitar + lead + piano; and asked for what comes next suggested as the chord suggestions are). **0.57.1:** speed, round one (the owner asked; three reviewers: audio, screen, waits; each finding checked; `tests/SpeedTests.cpp`). **0.57.2:** speed, round two (audio tracks keep their clips, VariAudio's signature kept, the MediaBay's search text kept). **0.57.3:** the spike report in the session log (the owner's clicks at 29% CPU, buffer 2048), the transport screens' shadow and figure widths kept. **0.57.4:** question boxes see-through (the owner: squared-off corners; as the progress box). **0.57.5:** the spike report per plug-in (the owner asked). **0.57.6:** the Audio Alignment panel's REFERENCE / TARGET lanes each with + and CLEAR (the owner asked: as the manual's panel). **0.58.0:** THE COMPOSER (the owner asked, after another idea generator: every layer stacked, each with a lock and a re-roll, one GENERATE). **0.59.0:** groups into groups (the owner's vocal buses: verse vox and chorus vox into John vox, into the master) and a composer lane dragged out as a MIDI file. **0.60.0:** routing as the other DAW's (the owner: any track anywhere but itself: outputs to groups or FX channels, FX outputs and sends, loops refused) and SETTINGS' DRAW WITHOUT THE GRAPHICS CARD (the owner's NEW froze inside the NVIDIA driver; not reproduced here). **0.61.0:** the composer's ROLL a new take of the same approach (the owner: the approach only from the style menu; GENERATE and ROLL keep the progression too), BUSY per lane, LOW STRING (bass, guitar) and a bass tuning. **0.62.0:** the Hub's INSTRUMENTS (profiles: range, tuning, frets, bend, keyswitches, velocity layers, the lane each is for; Djinn Bass 2 built in from the owner's screenshot, their tuning A0 D1 A1 D2 G2; since 0.82.2 A0 E1 A1 D2 G2) and the composer's string menu (any / low / ONLY STRING n with the profile's force-string keyswitch held). **0.62.1:** Shreddage Jupiter and Rogue on + NEW (the owner's: 7-string drop A, 24 frets, their velocity layers; `InstrumentProfile::shreddage`). **0.62.2:** from the owner's Jupiter manual (Shreddage 3.5: TACT articulations, Behavioral Keyswitches C-2..F#-2 force strings 1..7 (latching), G-2 off) and their map (Sustain C-1, Power Chords C#-1): `Keyswitch::forcesString` -1 = turns forcing off, `releaseKey()`, `playOnString (.., release)` presses it after the last note's start. Rogue the same as Jupiter (the owner confirmed: keyswitches, velocities, everything). **0.63.0:** power chords by keyswitch (the owner asked: a root alone after the power chord articulation C#-1, single notes back on C-1; the same velocity layers on both: the owner's): `Keyswitch::Use` none/powerChords/singleNotes (XML `use`), `keyUsedFor`, `usePowerChordKeys (idea, power, single)` (a group starting together of a root, its fifth and octaves: the root alone, the longest length, the loudest velocity; a key only where the kind changes and before the first; strums untouched), in `composeLayers` (guitar and lead, after humanize, before ONLY STRING; `playOnString` keeps the keys) and `LinesPage::getIdeas` (rhythm and lead: the LEAD profile else the GUITAR's); keyswitch notes not drawn on IDEAS' cards; InstrumentsPage's use box items 101/102 (`setKeyswitch` -101/-102). **0.63.1:** the owner: nothing shorter or faster than a 16th on Shreddage and Djinn, 16th chugs rare: `InstrumentProfile::shortestNote` (beats; default 0.25) / `chugRun` (default 0, guitars from XML 2), `keepPlayable (idea, shortest, chugRun, length)` (hits = notes together; a hit under `shortest - 0.04` after the last kept left out; lengths to `shortest` bounded by the next hit; muted (velocity < 60) hits a 16th apart at most `chugRun` in a row), before the power chords in `composeLayers` (bass/guitar/lead) and `LinesPage::getIdeas` (rhythm/lead/bass); InstrumentsPage SHORTEST NOTE / 16TH CHUGS IN A ROW boxes (`setShortestNote`, `setChugRun`). **0.64.0:** GROOVE (the owner's examples: Machine Head's Ten Ton Hammer, Kittie's Brackish: chugs answered by high dissonant stabs): `GuitarStyle::groove` (last; nu-metal's leads, beats, generator rules and fills), six riffs with stab characters `v` (tritone, base+24 and +30, vibrato) / `w` (base+22 and +24 bent a whole step) / `u` (base+26 ringing); `LineNote::Effect` none/vibrato/bend/ring (shapeRiff's palm mutes and busyness and `riffTake` leave effect notes alone); `Studio::partFromIdea` (every idea -> part: addLinePart, buildSection, auditionComposer, writeLineForDrag) and `effectControllers (notes, bendRange)` (bend: pitch bend 8192, ramp to a whole step by the profile's bend range (else 2) over half the note (0.25 beat at most), 8192 at its end; vibrato: CC 1 0, 0 at 30%, ramp to 96 at 80%, 0 at the end). Be honest about scale: Cubase took a large team decades. Say where it stands.

## Colours (the owner's rule, 0.5.1)

Only the family's colours: anything along the **red / pink / orange** gradient, **pearl white** and **pearl onyx** (the dark panels and greys). **Since 0.9.12 track and note colours are exactly the gradient's three: `pr7s::FamilyColours::red` `#D42E2A`, `pink` `#DE0639`, `orange` `#CD6619` (Project.h; `Finish::neonGradient` uses them).** No blue, green, violet, aqua or yellow anywhere (track colours included: `trackColour`, tested). PR7-G's signal-chain colours were removed from `ParagonColours`. The shared `LedDisplay` still has the plugins' green "in tune" for a tuner, which PR7-S never shows.

## The Cubase manual (0.5.1)

The owner wants Cubase **15**'s behaviour. Its manual is the **Cubase Pro 15 Help** on www.steinberg.help (allowed in this environment's network; archive.steinberg.help isn't). It's a Fluid Topics site with a JSON API:
- `curl https://www.steinberg.help/api/khub/maps` lists the manuals (Cubase Pro Help 15.0.30, English: map id `NlutMEjaYjAqk1bhdcAc5g`).
- `.../api/khub/maps/<map id>/toc` is the table of contents (nodes with `title`, `contentId`, `children`).
- `.../api/khub/maps/<map id>/topics/<contentId>/content` is a page's HTML.

Read the chapter before building a feature "as Cubase does", and quote it. 0.5.1 checked 0.1–0.5 against it. Still different, and left so on purpose:
- **Keys (the owner's choice: they stay):** R records (Cubase: R arms, Num * records), / cycles (Cubase: Num /), Home goes to the start (Cubase: Num . or ,), Escape closes the key editor (Cubase: Return opens/closes the editor).
- Only one track armed at a time; the export's "include the click" (our addition); the "<song> Audio" folder.

Cubase defaults it confirmed: Space, C, Delete, Ctrl+D, Ctrl+Z/Shift+Z, Ctrl+N/O/S/Shift+S, Ctrl+E, F3 (MixConsole), Alt+F3 (in the lower zone), Q, the key editor's tool keys 1/5/8, 120 ticks per sixteenth, middle C = C3, +6 dB fader top, the balance pan.

## Names

**Never** "Cubase", "Steinberg" or their graphics in the app, repo or release notes. "Inspired by Cubase's workflow" is fine. The look is the PR7 family's own.

**One exception (the owner agreed, 0.4.0):** ASIO's trademark credit, "ASIO is a trademark and software of Steinberg Media Technologies GmbH", in the release notes' install text, the README, and where ASIO is mentioned. Nothing more.

## Code layout

- `source/engine/`: no screen needed, fully testable.
  - `Project`: the song as plain data (tempo, signature, cycle, click, tracks, parts, notes in **beats** = quarter notes, each track's `inserts`, the master's volume and `masterInserts`), XML save/open, bar/beat formatting.
  - `Engine`: the audio device callback. Plays each track's events through its instrument (`AudioPluginInstance`), the cycle, the click, mix (volume, balance pan, mute, solo), live MIDI (devices via `MidiMessageCollector`, the on-screen `MidiKeyboardState`) into the live track, and records note on/off with their beats into a lock-free FIFO. **Threads:** the audio thread only `ScopedTryLock`s (a missed lock = one silent block); the message thread prepares everything outside the lock and swaps under it (`update`, `setInstrument`, `removeTrack`). No allocation on the audio thread. Signal path: instrument, its inserts in order, fader and pan, into the master buffer; master inserts, master fader, output; the click after the master. Effects are prepared stereo if they allow it, else as they are (mono in: L+R mixed; mono out: to both). Inserts by `insertEffect` / `removeEffect` (bus id 0 = master): 16 slots in fixed places (an `Insert` can be empty: `isEmpty()`; none after the last used), the first `preFaderSlots` before the fader and pan, the rest after (per track, and `masterPreFaderSlots`); the fader is applied to the track's own buffer, then the post-fader inserts, then the sum. Do sample work on raw pointers (FloatVectorOperations), not `AudioBuffer` methods: a buffer remembers being cleared and its own methods then skip work, even after something wrote to it through another view (it silenced mono effects once). Meters have two readers (0: transport and track list, 1: the mixer) so they don't take each other's peaks.
  - **Audio tracks:** `Track::Kind::audio` (input, stereoInput, monitor); their parts are clips (`Part::audioFile`, `audioOffset` seconds, `fadeIn`/`fadeOut` seconds; start/length in beats). `Engine::addAudioTrack`, clips played sample-accurately per segment of the block (`playClips`), input monitoring through the track's inserts, recording through a `ThreadedWriter` set with `setRecorder` (written on the audio thread, the first sample's song position in `getAudioRecordStart`; in a cycle every pass goes to the file and the clip plays the last complete one, as Cubase's Replace mode). `Studio`: recordings in `getAudioFolder()` ("<song> Audio" beside the song, else Documents/Paragon PR7-S/Unsaved Recordings/<time>; save copies them over), loaded whole and resampled to the engine's rate (`getAudio`, with peaks for drawing), latency compensation from the device (`setLatencyForTests`). `Engine::render` takes an input buffer for tests. Moving the position while stopped sets `rebase` (the clock restarts there even if play comes before a block).
  - **Export:** `Studio::beginExport` / `renderExport` / `endExport` (`exportMix` does all three). Begin takes the engine off the interface, re-prepares it at the export's rate, plays a copy of the song (no cycle, the click as asked, parts cut at the end so the ring-out holds only what's sounding; the ring-out goes on until 0.25 s below -100 dB, up to `tailSeconds`: 30 for the whole song, 0 for the cycle; 16, 24 or 32-bit float) and sets every plugin non-realtime (`Engine::setNonRealtime`); render runs on any thread (since 0.13.1 through `exportMix` and the renderRunner, as Render in Place); end puts everything back and deletes an unfinished file. `Windows::ExportPage` asks what (tests drive it directly; it's shown as a modal `DialogWindow`). `PARAGON_SNAPSHOT_EXPORT=1` draws it over the snapshot.
  - **Automation (0.6.0):** `AutomationLane` (points: beat and value 0–1, the control's travel; volume's is the fader's law `volumeToTravel`; mute steps, the rest ramp), on tracks and the master; R/W per track (`automationRead`/`Write`; W turns R on), Mute Automation per lane. The engine plays lanes with R on: plugin knobs once a block before the plugin, volume/pan/mute every 32 samples ramped, at the playhead when stopped; `getMixNow` for the faders. Studio: show/hide, append, `setLaneControl` (non-destructive: the old control's points kept hidden), remove, mute, points (turns R on), R/W all. Lane edits are undo steps; undo leaves what's shown and R/W. Removing or replacing an insert takes its lanes (from the undo history too). The manual's chapter: `Automation` in the TOC. **Writing:** `grabControl` / `releaseControl` (the UI calls them on a fader's or knob's drag start and end; mute's click does both), values through `setVolume`/`setPan`/`setMute`/`setMasterVolume`/`setKnob`; a `WritePass` per control; `Engine::setHeld` keeps a held control's lane out of playback; punch out on release (Touch: back to what was there over `returnSeconds`), stop, W off, a jump, the cycle coming round (`Engine::getCycleCount`); mute latches until stop. Plugin knobs: a `KnobWatcher` (AudioProcessorListener) per plugin, removed before the plugin is deleted (`unwatch` in effectGoing, instrument changes, track removal, new/open). **On screen:** `Timeline::layout` (rows: track, its shown lanes, MASTER and its lanes; laneHeight 48, masterHeight 40, our choice) for both views; `AutomationKeys` (R pink, W red, writing orange: only on the plain white finish; neon keys light pale gold as the other keys do); TrackList `LaneRow` / `MasterRow` / `Arrow`; ArrangeView paints lanes and edits points (`Drag::points/band/draw`, `PointSelection`, `commitPoints`: one undo step); MainView's header R/W, Alt+R/W, Delete for points, Esc to cancel. The master's row is our design (the manual doesn't describe an output channel track).
  - **Sends, FX and group channels (0.7.0):** `Track::Kind::fx` / `group` (`isChannel()`: no parts, no instrument, never armed). `Track::output` (0 master, or a group or FX channel's id; since 0.60.0 any track that makes sound to any group or FX channel, never in a loop: `Project::canRouteTo`), `Track::sends` (up to `Project::maxSends` 8, by slot: `Send` target FX id, levelDb, on, preFader) and `sendsBypass`. Engine: `addChannel`; roles source/group/fx processed in that order; each channel's `bus` collects what's routed or sent to it; pre-fader sends after the pre-fader inserts, post-fader after the fader, pan and post inserts (`runSends`, gain ramped per block); solo of a track never silences channels. Automation `Target::send` (slot = send). Studio: `addFxChannel` ("FX n-<effect>"), `addGroupChannel`, `setOutput`, `setSend`, `setSendLevel` (writes automation like a fader), `removeSend`, `setSendsBypass`; removing a channel resets what pointed at it; `Project::fromXml` resets routing that leads nowhere. UI: add-track menu, channel rows, track menu Output; MixerView INSERTS/SENDS rack toggle (our design), `SendSlot`, OUT button. Manual pages: Send Effects, Effect Channel Tracks, Group Channel Tracks (TOC). Defaults that are our choice: 8 sends, new send post-fader at 0 dB.
  - **MIDI file import (0.7.1):** `MidiImport.h/.cpp`: `readMidiFile` (JUCE `MidiFile`; type 0 split by channel; ticks per quarter or SMPTE; the first tempo and time signature; tracks without notes left out). `Studio::importMidi` (new song: the file's tempo and signature, the empty default track removed, no undo before it; else into this song at a beat, its tempo kept): a Paragon Synth track per imported track, one part over whole bars. MainView: IMPORT button (asks new song or this one when the song has parts) and `FileDragAndDropTarget` (a .mid at the bar dropped on). Manual pages: Importing Audio and MIDI Files > MIDI File Import. Defaults that are our choice: see the 0.7.1 notes.
  - `ParagonSynth`: the built-in instrument (an `AudioPluginInstance`, so it's played like a VST3).
  - `PluginHost`: VST3 format manager and the known-plugin list (kept in the settings file); `getInstruments` / `getEffects`, `create` / `createEffect`.
  - `Project` also has `quantize` (note starts to the *song's* grid; same-pitch twins merge) and `noteName` (middle C = C3, as in Cubase).
  - `Studio`: message-thread controller; owns project, engine, host, `AudioDeviceManager`, settings; every action; turns recordings into parts; gives every part an `id` (not saved; `findPart`) so the key editor keeps its part when indices change; `setNotes` replaces a part's notes (one undo step per editor gesture); the mixer's actions (`addInsert`, `replaceInsert`, `removeInsert`, `setInsertBypass`, `setMasterVolume`; `onEffectGoing` closes an effect's window before it's deleted; `setVolume`/`setPan` with `undoable = false` while a fader is dragged); adding a track records an undo state (so the next change to it can be undone); undo (parts, song settings, track names and mix; not adding/removing tracks); `onInstrumentGoing` lets the UI close an instrument's window **before** the instance is deleted (a plugin editor must never outlive its processor).
- `source/ui/`: `MainView` (header, `TransportBar`, `TrackList` | `ArrangeView`, and the lower zone below them: `KeyEditor` or `MixerView`, one docked at a time, either in its own window instead; MainView's `Zone`s handle both; keys go to the editor after a click in it; no on-screen keyboard since 0.3.0), `Windows` (instrument editor, settings, plugins), `Pearl.h` (the pearl unit surface). From PR7-C: `ParagonLookAndFeel`, `Finish` (settings file "Paragon PR7-S"), `LedDisplay`, `Logo` (the mark with "-S"), `LevelMeter`.
  - `MixerView`: a `Strip` per track and the master's (16 insert slots, pan, M/S/R/W, fader following automation while R is on, with the PR7 fader look lit from the bottom, meter). Insert menus are async popups (not testable directly; the studio actions are).
  - `LevelMeter`: PR7-C's meter (segments every 3 px, `ParagonColours::meterColour` orange to pink-red, held peak 1.5 s), dBFS -60 to +6. `paintSegments` and `Ballistics` are shared by the transport's OUT, the track list (horizontal) and the mixer.
  - `Windows::ZoneWindow`: the key editor or the mixer in its own window (WINDOW / DOCK). Since 0.9.10 (the owner's keys): F2 / F3 open or close the MIDI editor / mixer where they were, Alt+F2 / Alt+F3 move them between docked and full screen (`toggleZone` / `toggleZonePlace`); `toggleMixerWindow` / `toggleMixerDocked` remain for code and tests. Shows the editor without owning it; MainView moves it in and out (`setKeyEditorInWindow`), remembers the choice and the window's state in the settings file. The window is never deleted inside its own click (callAsync).
  - **Ctrl in tests is `commandModifier`** (the app reads Ctrl with `isCommandDown()`: Cmd on a Mac). `ctrlModifier` is the Mac's Control key: 0.32.0's first build failed on macOS only for that.
  - Tests with a real window: don't expect sizes bigger than the screen (macOS keeps a window within the screen's height; CI's screens are small). 0.2.1 failed on macOS CI for that and was never released.
  - Tests on Linux register the window manager's atoms first (`tests/TestMain.cpp`): with no window manager on the virtual display, JUCE fails creating a real window.
  - **Getting around (0.7.2):** `Timeline::autoScroll` / `scrollLength` (8 bars past the last part, at least 32: our choice) / `zoomStep` (1.5, our choice); `ArrangeView::zoom` / `zoomFull` / `followPlayhead` (Page Scroll: playing, or a jump while stopped); the key editor has its own (`KeyEditor::zoom`, `setAutoScroll`, its scroll bars inside it: `scrollBarSize`). MainView: G/H/F/Shift+F routed as the other keys (to the editor after a click in it), the arrangement's scroll bars (`refreshScrollBars`, `scrollBarMoved`), `ZoneDivider` and `setDockedHeight` (each zone's `dockedHeight`, settings `keyEditorHeight` / `mixerHeight`, between `minimumHeight` and `minimumArrangeHeight`); TrackList's AUTO-SCROLL key; settings `arrangeAutoScroll`, `keyEditorAutoScroll`, `mixerRack`. MixerView `setRackShown` (the lit INSERTS/SENDS clicked again), `getMinimumHeight`, `onMinimumHeightChange`. `ParagonLookAndFeel::drawScrollbar` (JUCE's own is blue). **0.7.3:** `AutoScroll` (on, stationary, suspendWhenEditing, suspended; `follow`, `toggle`, `refreshKey`, `showMenu`, `load`/`save` with prefix "arrange" / "keyEditor") in `Timeline::autoScroll` and the key editor; a click in the event display while playing suspends (ArrangeView/KeyEditor `mouseDown`), `followPlayhead` clears it when stopped. Vertical zoom: `Timeline::rowZoom` (0.5 to 3, step 1.25; `trackRowHeight` / `laneRowHeight` / `masterRowHeight`; `layout` and `contentHeight` are members now: use `timeline.layout (studio)`), `ArrangeView::zoomRows`; a track row under 66 px shows one line (TrackList::Row). Key editor `rowHeight` (6 to 36), `zoomRows`. `ParagonLookAndFeel::menuArrowProperty` draws a menu key's arrow (the fonts have none). Zooms aren't saved. Manual pages: Zooming in the Project Window, Zoom Submenu, Auto-Scroll Settings Menu, Suspend Auto-Scroll When Editing, Setting the Track Height.
  - **Editing tools (0.8.0):** `Part::muted` (saved; the engine skips muted parts' notes and clips). Studio `splitPart` (MIDI notes across the cut split in two: the manual's Split MIDI Events, our choice; an audio clip's second half's `audioOffset` carries on, fade-in with the first, fade-out with the second), `splitPartRepeatedly`, `splitAtCursor` (all parts the playhead is in, or one part id), `glueParts` (to the next by start, or all after; MIDI: the heard notes in place; audio: only a clip and the next cut from the same file end to end, else false), `setPartMuted` / `setPartsMuted`, `deletePartsFrom`; each one undo step. `ArrangeView::Tool` (`setTool`, `nextTool`, `toolDown` / `toolUp`, Drag `newPart` / `muteBand` / `zoomBand` / `play`, the split line `hover`), `ArrangeTools` (the row; MainView `toolbarHeight` 32). SELECT's Alt-click splits (no drag), Alt-drag on an empty instrument track draws. MainView routes 1, 3–9 (not after a click in the key editor: its own 1/5/8), F9/F10, Alt+X. The docked mixer's default height is at least `MixerView::getPreferredHeight` until dragged. Manual pages: Project Window Toolbar, Splitting Events (and Repeatedly), Gluing Events Together, Muting Events, Removing Events, Creating Empty MIDI Parts, Selecting with the Object Selection Tool. Our design: PLAY plays the song while held and returns; the Erase tool's Alt (all after it); MUTE's rectangle mutes all it touches unless all are muted.
  - **Range Selection (0.8.1):** `Studio::Range` (start, end, trackIds) and `RangeClip` (per track, parts from the range's start; origin); `splitRange`, `cropRange`, `deleteRange` (closeGap: Delete Time), `copyRange`, `cutRange`, `insertSilence`, `pasteRange` (from a track down, audio only onto audio tracks; `insertTime`: Paste Time), `moveRange` (copy: Alt), `duplicateRange`; helpers `splitTrackAt`, `slice`, `shiftFrom` (all through `cutPart`, so notes across an edge split). ArrangeView `Tool::range`, `range` / `setRange`, `rangeCommand`, `rangeClip`, Drag `rangeNew` / `rangeStart` / `rangeEnd` / `rangeMove`, `rangeDoubleClick` (a part, or the gap), `showRangeMenu`, `paintRange`. MainView routes the range keys before the key editor's (unless the last click was in it); `rangeCopiedLast` decides what Ctrl+V pastes. The tools ignore right-clicks (`isPopupMenu`): the part's menu always. Manual chapter: Range Editing in the Project Window and in the Editors. Our choices: a click alone clears the range; paste on the selected track; automation isn't moved by Delete Time / Insert Silence.
  - **Locators and tracks (0.8.2):** ArrangeView ruler: Ctrl-click left, Alt-click right, Ctrl+Alt both (one grid step), `Drag::cycleLeft` / `cycleRight` (the handles, 6 px), cursors; `locatorsToSelection` (P). MainView: number pad 1/2 (Ctrl: set) checked before the Ctrl shortcuts and the tool digits (the pad types digits), P / Alt+P, Up/Down (the first part on the track if a part was selected), Shift+Delete. Studio: `selectedTracks` with `selectedTrack` (the one clicked last) in it; `toggleTrackSelected`, `selectTrackRun`, `isTrackSelected`, `getSelectedTracks`; `moveTracks` (undoable: `restore` puts the tracks' order back), `duplicateTrack(s)` (states from the running instrument and effects; name + " copy"), `removeTracks`, `isTrackEmpty`, `getEmptyTracks`. TrackList: Row Ctrl/Shift/drag (`dragTrack` / `dropTracks`, the line in `paintOverChildren`), `moveSelectedTracks`, `removeSelectedTracks` (asks if not empty), `addTrackLikeSelected` (double-click), menu items 50–56. Window: `Windows::spanOf` / `allScreensArea` (the strip the screens share), MainView `toggleSpanAllScreens` / `isSpanningAllScreens` (SETTINGS' button), Main.cpp keeps the window's state ("mainWindow"; max 32768 wide). Manual pages: Left and Right Locators (and its children), Reordering Tracks, Selecting Tracks (with Arrow Keys), Duplicating Tracks, Removing Selected / Empty Tracks, Adding Tracks via Double-Click.
  - **0.8.3:** `Studio::importAudio` / `isAudioFile` (WAV/AIFF copied, FLAC/Ogg/MP3 written as 24-bit WAV into `getAudioFolder()`, unique names; clips one after another; a new audio track, stereo if the first file is, unless the target is an audio track; one undo step for the clips); MainView IMPORT menu (`importMidiFile` / `importAudioFiles`), `filesDropped` (audio onto the track under the drop, at the bar). JUCE 9 reads MP3 (JUCE_USE_MP3AUDIOFORMAT is on by default); `tests/data/tone.mp3` (an ffmpeg 440 Hz tone, peak -21.5 dB), `PR7S_TEST_DATA`. CPU: `Engine::notePerformance` (the device callback's time against the block's: an average smoothed 0.9/0.1, the peak since `takePerformance`, `overloads` at load >= 1), TransportBar's CPU meter beside OUT (`followPerformance`, a click resets, tooltip). Wide: `MainView::wideWidth` 2400, `TransportBar::setToolsWidth` (the group keys + position screen `widePositionWidth` 540 + tempo + signature + tools centred together, `getToolsArea`), `ArrangeTools::setOnUnit` / `unitWidth` (62 px keys, TOOLS stamped), the header's keys shifted to the middle. Manual pages: Audio File Import (Importing Audio Files, Import Options), Audio Performance Monitor Panel. `PARAGON_SNAPSHOT_VIEW=5760x1000` renders the wide layout (run xvfb with a big enough screen).
  - **0.8.4:** `Studio::renderInPlace` (`RenderOptions` mode / processing / tail / bits / source, `RenderSource` track + stretch + wholeTrack): each stretch through `exportMix` with `ExportOptions::prepareSong` (only the source's parts in it, the others' cleared; Dry: inserts bypassed, sends and output cleared; Dry/Channel: fader and pan at 0 and their lanes dropped; not +Master: master inserts bypassed, master fader 0 dB), clips on a new stereo audio track moved under the source (Dry: copies of the effects with their states, sends, output; Dry/Channel: the fader and pan). `Engine::settleMix` at every export's start (after `setNonRealtime`, which since 0.8.6 releases and re-prepares each plugin switching mode: a VST3 reads realtime/offline in setupProcessing, and JUCE skips that when rate and block size are unchanged; Kontakt and SD3 rendered silence; test `ModeMinded`) (the faders' SmoothedValues, the sends' lastGain, the master: no glide from the last playback; it was audible in renders). `Windows::RenderPage` (settings keys render*; `keptOptions`), MainView `renderInPlace` (menus of part, range, track: "as last time" skips the page). `ToolIcons.h` (paths in 24x24; `draw`, `cursor`; ArrangeView caches the cursors, not a static: they mustn't outlive JUCE), `ToolKey`. Lit pedal keys on neon finishes drawn as black keys (drawButtonBackground/Text: `neonKeys() && ! on`; since 0.48.0 every key is black). TransportBar: `autoScrollArea` (MainView's `autoScrollKey` / `autoScrollMenu`), `cpuScreen` (an LED screen), wide sizes (keys 66, position 440, tools 9x64 with no label) so the group fits 1920. TrackList: + ADD TRACK in the header. Ruler: Ctrl/Alt anywhere. MainView `wasWide`: turning wide opens the mixer docked at its least, racks hidden. LookAndFeel colours for ComboBox, PopupMenu, TextEditor, AlertWindow, ListBox. Manual pages: Rendering Audio and MIDI (Render Tracks / Render Selection dialogs).
  - **0.8.5 (the buffer size):** `AudioAdvice` (engine): `firstDeviceType` (ASIO when installed, used by Studio only when there are no saved settings; JUCE starts on Windows Audio, whose shared mode has one buffer size), `bufferText` (SETTINGS' line per driver type), `typesWithDevices`. `Studio::reloadAudioDevice` (close + `restartLastAudioDevice`: the driver asked afresh). SettingsContent: the selector in a `Viewport` (it sets its own height), the buffer line, RELOAD DRIVER (devices with a control panel), listens to the device manager. Tests: `tests/DeviceTests.h` `FakeDriver` (a named type, reported sizes, `panelSize`), `"[snapshot-settings]"` (`PARAGON_SNAPSHOT_DRIVER`). JUCE facts: ASIO lists min..max from the driver (powers of two if granularity < 0) plus its preferred size; WASAPI shared: the default size only; exclusive: 64..2048; `Button::triggerClick` is async (tests call `onClick`).
  - **The Inspector (0.9.0):** `Inspector` (ui): `width` 210, left of the track list for the whole height (beside the docked zone; the manual's left zone), sections `Section` basics/routing/inserts/sends/fader in a viewport (`Header` folds; settings `inspectorFolded`, `inspectorShown`), shows `getSelectedTrack()`. Fader: `MixerView (studio, true)` (single-strip mode: `setSingleTrack`, no bar/master/rack, meter reader 2; also the owner of the insert/send/output menus and the `SendSlot`s). `TrackList::showInstrumentMenu` is public (the instrument key). MainView `toggleInspector` (INSPECTOR key, Ctrl+Alt+L: the manual's Show/Hide Left Zone, Window Zones category). `ParagonLookAndFeel::barFromCentreProperty` (pan). Routing: `Track::midiInput` (device name, empty: all) and `midiChannel` (1-16, saved when not default, restored by undo); `Studio::setMidiInput` / `setMidiChannel`; Engine events and live MIDI on the track's channel (status & 0xf0 checks), `stopHeldNotes` on it; `Engine::getLiveInput` (the devices' callback: `LiveInput` drops devices the live track doesn't listen to; `setLiveInputFilter`, `acceptsLiveFrom`), set in `updateLiveTrack`. Track meters have 3 readers. `PARAGON_SNAPSHOT_INSPECTOR=fader|none`. Manual pages: Inspector, Inspector Sections, Track Inspector Sections (Basics Section for Instrument Tracks), Window Zones Category.
  - **Disable / hide (0.9.1):** `Track::disabled` / `hidden` (saved, not undo steps). `Studio::setTracksDisabled` (states kept from the running plugins, effects removed from the engine (`effectGoing` first), instrument set to nullptr; enable: `host->create` / `createEffect` from the kept states; not channels; disarms; `setArmed`/`selectTrack` won't arm a disabled one), `open` doesn't load a disabled track's plugins, Engine `update`: disabled = silent, no events/clips/lanes, no monitor. `setTracksHidden` (selection moves to a shown track), `isTrackShown`, `getShownTracks`. Hidden: `Timeline::layout` skips them (so the track list, arrangement, lanes), ArrangeView `trackIndex`/`tracksBetween` over shown tracks, `tracksFrom` (paste) skips them, Up/Down, MixerView skips hidden and disabled (our choice: the manual's "sync" always on). Disabled drawn grey (0xff4a454c). TrackList menu 59 (Disable/Enable), 60 (Hide). Inspector tabs (`Tab`, settings `leftZoneTab`), `VisibilityRow` (ToggleButton per track), SHOW ALL, `enable` key. `RenderOptions::Source::disable`, `hideSource` (settings `renderHide`). `PARAGON_SNAPSHOT_VISIBILITY=1`. Manual pages: Disabling Tracks, Track Visibility, Render Tracks Dialog (Source Track Settings).
  - **0.9.2:** **Redraws:** ArrangeView's and KeyEditor's timers repaint only the playhead's old and new strips when it moved (`refreshPlayhead`, `playheadX`); paint skips rows/parts/notes outside `g.getClipBounds()` (and works out a row's top once per track, not per part); the split tool's hover repaints strips; TransportBar repaints its screens only when they change; the Inspector lists MIDI devices only when MIDI IN opens (`DeviceBox::beforePopup`). Never repaint a whole big view from a timer: the owner's window spans three screens. `tests/PerfTests.cpp` `"[perf]"` (hidden) times it. **Several parts:** ArrangeView `alsoSelected` (ids besides `selection`, the primary the key editor follows), `getSelectedParts`, `isPartSelected`, `selectParts`, `selectAllParts`, `copySelected`, `pasteParts` (atOrigin), `partsClip` (a `RangeClip` from `Studio::copyParts`, pasted with `pasteRange`), Drag `marquee`; Shift-click toggles; dragging a selected part moves all (`Studio::moveParts`: beats and shown-track delta, same kind else its own track); `Studio::deleteParts`. MainView `copiedLast` (range / notes / parts) decides Ctrl+V; Ctrl+C/X go to the parts unless the last click was in the key editor; Alt+V Paste at Origin; Ctrl+A. **Inspector:** MainView `InspectorEdge`, `setInspectorWidth` (180-560, settings `inspectorWidth`); INSERTS in `insertsView` (5 in view, `insertsInView`). **TrackList rows:** no instrument key (hidden), name `titleFont (22)` on the left, keys in two rows on the right. Manual pages: Selecting with the Object Selection Tool, Select Submenu, Moving Events with the Object Selection Tool, Pasting Events, Duplicating Events, Removing Events.
  - **0.9.3:** `ScaledHolder` (ui): the key editor lives in `keyEditorHolder` (MainView's `editorZone` content, `getKeyEditorFrame`), laid out at the frame's size / scale and drawn with `setTransform (scale)`, so everything in it doubles and its own coordinates stay as they were (tests at 100%: `setKeyEditorSize (100)`). `setKeyEditorSize` (100/150/200, settings `keyEditorSize`, default 200; the constructor sets only the frame: the window isn't laid out yet), the editor's `sizeBox` / `onSizeChange` / `setSizeShown`, docked minimum height scales. KeyEditor `Tool::split` (key 3, `splitAt`: the clicked note, or all selected it crosses; snapped unless Ctrl), `penCursor` / `scissorsCursor` from `ToolIcons::cursor`, grids 1/1 and 1/2. Manual pages: Key Editor Toolbar, Splitting Note Events.
  - **0.9.4 (random velocities, the owner's idea, not in the manual):** KeyEditor `randomizeVelocities` (selected notes only; one edit; the selection kept), `velocityRanges` (settings `velocityRange<i>Low/High`, default 40-70 / 70-100 / 100-127), `setVelocityRange` (clamped 1-127, swapped), `velocityKeys` (LOW/MID/HIGH with the range on them) and `velocityRangesKey` (a CallOutBox of three TwoValueHorizontal sliders) in one panel row (the panel must still fit docked at 200%). Keys Alt+B/N/M in the editor and in MainView's `editorsAnyway`.
  - **Freeze and track presets (0.9.8):** `Track::frozen` / `freezeFile` (relative to `getAudioFolder()`, "Freeze/<name> <id>.wav"; saved). `Studio::freezeTrack (id, tailSeconds, error)`: an enabled instrument or audio track with parts; `exportMix` with `prepareSong` (other tracks' parts cleared; its fader/pan 0, mute off, sends and output cleared and those lanes dropped; master inserts bypassed, master 0 dB, `masterRead` off), 32-bit float; then `unloadPlugins` (shared with disable), disarmed, `frozenParts[id]` kept. `changed()` puts frozen tracks' parts back from `frozenParts` first (the lock); `changeInstrument`/`addInsert`/`replaceInsert`/`removeInsert` refuse frozen tracks, `setArmed`/`selectTrack` don't arm them. Engine `update`: a frozen track = one clip of its file from 0, no events, no monitoring. `unfreezeTrack (id, deleteFile)`: `loadPlugins`. `open` doesn't load frozen tracks' plugins, refills `frozenParts`; `save` copies freeze files. `endExport` now calls `settleMix` (the first playback after any render glided from the render's levels). Presets: `makeTrackPreset` (a one-track Project XML with attribute `trackPreset`; plugin states from the running instances; no parts, automation, sends, output or flags), `applyTrackPreset` (same kind only, not frozen/disabled; volume/pan/colour/QCs/MIDI channel/pre-fader slots/input applied to the undo states too: not undoable, as the manual; name and parts kept), `addTrackFromPreset` (a track of its kind, the preset's name, under the selected one), `trackPresetFolder()` (Documents/Paragon PR7-S/Track Presets), `.pr7track`. UI: `TrackActions` (ui; menu ids 70-78: freeze tails 0/2/5/10/30 s, unfreeze keep/delete, save/load preset; `freezeSelected`, `unfreezeSelected`; `savePresetTo` / `loadPresetFrom` / `addTrackFrom` for tests; choosers via `callAsync`), TrackList menu (Render in Place and Change instrument off when frozen) and + ADD TRACK "Track from a preset...", Inspector `freeze` / `preset` keys in Basics (none on a group; FX: PRESET only; REC disabled when frozen), FROZEN under the row's name and on its parts (pearl-white veil). `PARAGON_SNAPSHOT_FREEZE=1`. Manual pages: Freeze Channel Options Dialog for Instruments, Unfreeze Channel Options Dialog for Instruments, Creating a Track Preset, Applying Track Presets. Our choices: inserts always in, the instrument always unloaded (the manual's options), post-fader inserts rendered before the live fader, one preset folder (the manual: per kind), no multi-track presets or MediaBay, tempo changes after freezing aren't followed.
  - **0.9.9:** **Never call a plugin's `hasEditor()` on a refresh path:** JUCE's VST3 answers by creating the editor's view (Kontakt: slow; every click stuck ~1 s). Use `Studio::hasEditor` (asked once per instance, `editorAnswers`, forgotten in `onPluginGoing`). **The screen never takes the engine's lock to read:** `getInstrument`, `getEffect`, `getNumEffects`, `getSendNowDb`, `takeTrackPeak`, `getMixNow` read atomics and the track/slot lists only the message thread changes (message thread only); the lock is for changes. Renders: `Engine::setNonRealtime (offline, sounding)` switches and prepares only the plugins that switch, offline only those of `sounding` tracks (beginExport: tracks with unmuted parts in the played copy, the channels their output and sends reach, the master's); `Studio::beginBatch` / `endBatch` (Render in Place, `TrackActions::freezeSelected`) keep plugins offline and the device off across several exports; `endExport` settles the mix. Solo: `setTracksHidden` / `setTracksDisabled` clear solo, the engine ignores a disabled track's solo, Render in Place moves a soloed source's solo to the new track when the source is muted/hidden/disabled/removed; `setSolo (id, on, exclusive)` (Ctrl-click S), `anySolo` / `anyMute`, `deactivateAllSolo` / `deactivateAllMute` (TrackList header `muteAll` / `soloAll`, the manual's Deactivate All Mute/Solo States). TrackList rows: no M S REC R W (kept as hidden members), the state written under the name. `ValueSlider` (ui/ValueSlider.h): double-click opens a CallOutBox to type (`parse`, `enter`: one setValue, one undo step; `ValueEntry::parseDb` / `parsePan`), Ctrl-click resets (setDoubleClickReturnValue with commandModifier); mixer strips (and their painted readouts, `Strip::mouseDoubleClick`), the Inspector (readout Labels editable on double-click), TrackList's volume. Tests can't run JUCE's message loop: close CallOutBoxes synchronously (exitModalState + setVisible false), not `dismiss()`. `tests/PerfTests.cpp` has a heavy-plugin-on-an-audio-thread case. `PARAGON_SNAPSHOT_STATES=1`. Manual page: Using Solo and Mute (Fader Section).
  - **0.9.10:** `ParagonLookAndFeel::drawAlertBox` (black pearl, red glow edge, white Bebas, `Logo::drawMark` for any icon) and the alert fonts; `"[snapshot-alert]"`. TransportBar: `positionScreen` and `timeScreen` share the old width (`widePositionWidth` is both plus the 12 px gap), `drawFixedPitch` (Bebas figures in fixed cells). MainView: the editor/mixer/Inspector keys are hidden members (Zone needs its button); F1 `toggleInspector`, F2/F3 `toggleZone`, Alt+F2/F3 `toggleZonePlace` (docked <-> window, the window set to the display's userArea); "MIDI Editor" in names (settings keys still `keyEditor*`); `snapButton` / `quantizeButton` (`showQuantizeMenu`, `quantizeName`) after R W, J toggles snap. `Project::snapOn` / `quantizeBeats` (saved as `snap` / `quantize`, not undo steps; `Studio::setSnap` / `setQuantizeBeats`). `Timeline::grid` is the quantize value (a step: locators), `snapGrid` what things snap to (0 with snap off; guard divisions). Pedal keys with `menuArrowProperty` and text draw the text with the arrow on the right. KeyEditor: `sideWidth` 150; the panel's controls in `panelColumn` inside `panelView` (a Viewport), `layoutColumn` (one column, `columnKeyHeight` 24), `InfoView` / `paintInfo` (the selection's text at its foot), `panelHeaderHeight`. Velocity handles: `getVelocityBox`, `velocityHandleAt`, `VelocityHandle` (move, scale, tiltLeft, tiltRight, scaleCentre), `Gesture::velocityHandle`, `dragVelocityHandle` (from `original`, one `commit`). Manual pages: Snap Function, Grid Type Menu, Snap Grid, Project Window Toolbar, Controller Event Editor, Editing Velocity Events. Our choices: grid = quantize always (no Grid Type menu), the key editor keeps its own grid, no Alt handle variants or Stretch.
  - **0.9.11:** **PR7-K, the studio's synth:** `Instrument::Kind::paragonSynth` (name kept so songs load) now means PR7-K; `PluginHost::getStudioSynth` (beside the app: `currentApplicationFile`'s folder, then the VST3 default folders; the newest version; cached; not looked for when `findStudioSynth` is false, which Studio(false) sets so tests use `ParagonSynth`), `create` falls back to `ParagonSynth` when PR7-K is missing or fails; `isStudioSynthFound` labels the menus. CI's release job downloads PR7-K's latest `*-VST3.zip` (gh release download --repo ARYEZ/Paragon-PR7-K) into each artifact folder, `pack` zips the app with `Paragon PR7-K.vst3`; a missing PR7-K is a warning, not a failure. The cycle strip uses `Finish::neonGradient`. ArrangeView `splitLineX` (the snapped line; repaint its strip, not the mouse's). `toggleZonePlace` sets the window to `Windows::allScreensArea()`. `Studio::saveInBackground` (prepareSave on the message thread: states and audio copies; a Project copy's toXml + writeTo on `saver`, a 1-thread ThreadPool; marked saved at once, unsaved again on failure; `saveAlive` guards the call back; the destructor waits), `isSaving`, `waitForSaves` (tests); MainView SAVE / SAVE AS use it; `save` stays synchronous. `paintPart`: body at alpha 0.05 with a 1 px edge at 0.45 (was 0.32). TrackList: `addTrack` hidden; `makeAddTrackMenu (offset)` / `addTrackChosen`, the track menu's "Add Track" submenu (`addTrackOffset` 1000000), `mouseDown` right-click under the rows. Perf tests: saving with large states (~19 ms/MB: toXml a third, writeTo two thirds).
  - **0.9.12:** `Windows::Instrument` (plug-in windows): a Timer sets `setAlwaysOnTop (Process::isForegroundProcess())` (they went behind the spanning main window and looked closed), `onKey` forwards keys to MainView::keyPressed. `FamilyColours` (Project.h): `trackColour` cycles red, pink, orange; `fromXml` maps retired colours first, then `nearest`. KeyEditor `velocityColour` (below MID's low: orange; below HIGH's low: pink; else red) for notes and bars. Playheads (ArrangeView, KeyEditor): pearl white 0xfff4eeea line, pink glow (two widths), red while recording. TrackList `alphabetical` (the track menu sorted, separators dropped; audio inputs in a "Record From" submenu).   - **The tempo track (0.10.0):** `TempoMap` (engine/TempoMap.h: `TempoEvent` beat/bpm/ramp, the ramp from the event before; segments with exact ramps: tempo even in beats, seconds by a logarithm; `secondsAt`, `beatAt`, `bpmAt`, `secondsBetween`, `beatAfter`). `Project::tempoTrackOn`, `tempoEvents` (first at 0), `tempoTrackShown`, `tempoLocked`, `newTempoType` (ramp/step/automatic), `tempoViewLow/High`; `tempoMap()` (the fixed `bpm` when off), `bpmAt`, `followsTempoTrack`; saved as a `Tempo` element; undo restores on/events/shown. **Never use `secondsPerBeat()` for anything placed in the song**: use the map (it's only the fixed tempo's). Engine: `Settings::tempo` + `tempoVersion` (the clock re-bases when the map changes), `Timing` (sampleAt/beatAt) in schedule, playClips, the click, recording, automation spans, the playhead's bpm. Studio: `addTempoTrack` (on, one event), `removeTempoTrack` (hidden, off, events kept), `setTempoTrackOn`, `addTempoEvent` (type by newTempoType; at an event's beat: its tempo), `setTempoEvents`, `removeTempoEvents` (never the first), `setTempoAtCursor`, `insertTempoEventAtCursor`, `setTempoLocked`, `setNewTempoType`, `setTempoView`, `getTempoNow`; cutPart/slice/splitTrackAt take the map; recordings, imports, renders, fades by the map.   - **Key commands and macros (0.11.0):** `KeyCommands` (ui/KeyCommands.h): `Command` (id like "Transport.Click", category, name, `defaults`, `action`), `Macro` (name, command ids), `Map` (`add`, `keysFor`, `isCustomized`, `assign` (takes the key from its owner, returns it), `removeKey`, `reset`, `resetAll` (macros kept), `clearAll`, `commandFor`, `handle` (a key of the owner's: run it; a default taken away: swallowed; else false), macros (`addMacro`, `renameMacro` (keys and references follow), `duplicateMacro`, `deleteMacro`, `addToMacro (after)`, `removeFromMacro`, `moveInMacro`; ids `Macro.<name>`; `run` stops at depth 5), `toXml`/`fromXml` (only customised commands and the macros; settings key `keyCommands`; presets `.pr7keys` in Documents/Paragon PR7-S/Key Commands), `toHtml`), `describe`/`parse` ("Ctrl+Alt+F13"), `normalise`.   - **0.12.1 (renders off the message thread):** `JUCE_MODAL_LOOPS_PERMITTED=1`. Studio `renderRunner` (unset: inline, as the tests; MainView sets it only when a JUCEApplication runs: a `std::thread` does the work while the message thread runs `runDispatchLoopUntil (30)` and `reportRenderProgress`; the `ProgressBox` is modal meanwhile), `isRendering` (Main's close and quit wait; Studio's timer skips), `renderDone` (atomic, written by the render's thread; `onProgress` only from the message thread). `beginExport` / `endExport` (plugin set-up) stay on the message thread; only `renderExport` moves. **Never render on the message thread:** Kontakt-like instruments rendering offline wait for their loader threads, which can wait for the message thread.

Studio `processTempo (start, end, newSeconds, factor)` / `tempoScaling`: events in the range scaled by one factor (exact over ramps); edges held by events at the start (a ramp arriving there: an event a 960th of a beat before it) and a 960th after the end (back on the old curve: `before.bpmAt (end + just)`); refuses outside 20-300 BPM; one undo step. MainView `toggleTempoEditor` (Ctrl+T in handleKey's Ctrl switch; command Window.TempoEditor), ArrangeView / TransportBar `onOpenTempoEditor` (their tempo menus). `"[snapshot-tempo-editor]"`.   - **The MediaBay (0.21.0):** engine `MediaBay.h/.cpp`: `MediaType` audio/midi/trackPreset/song (`mediaTypeOf` by extension, `mediaTypeName`), `MediaItem` (file, name, size, modified, seconds, sampleRate, channels, bpm, key, beats, notes, rating; `searchText`: name, file name, folder name, type, tempo, key, lower case), `TextSearch` (tokens, a small parser: or (`or`, `,`) of and (`and`, `+`, nothing) of factors (`not`, a leading `-`, parentheses, words; "phrases"); words matched with `contains`; a minus inside a word is the word), `MediaQuery` (text, types, minimumRating, folder, subfolders), `MediaColumn`, `MediaDatabase (store)` (locations; `rescan` on a `Scanner` thread (`scanAll`: RangedDirectoryIterator per location, unchanged files (size + date) not read again, waits while `shouldPause`), `scanNow` (tests), `takeScanResults` (message thread; items sorted by name; saves), `find`, `sort`, `shuffle (seed)`, `setRating` / `getRating` (by path, kept when the file isn't scanned), `read` (audio header; MIDI via `readMidiFile`: beats, notes, its tempo), `bpmFromName` (2-3 digits beside "bpm", 40-300), `keyFromName` (a name part: capital A-G, # or b, then m/min/minor/maj/major or nothing; "bass" isn't Bb), `save` / `load` (XML: Location, Rating, Item); `pendingLock` guards `items`/`locations` against the scanner). Engine `setPreview (buffer, loop)` (swapped under the lock; the old one freed on the message thread), `stopPreview`, `setPreviewGain`, `setPreviewLoop`, `isPreviewPlaying`, `getPreviewProgress`; mixed after the click. Studio `getMediaBay` (lazy; store "MediaBay.xml" beside the settings; first time Documents/Paragon PR7-S a location; `shouldPause` = playing or recording), `readAudio (File)` (uncached; `getAudio` uses it), `previewMedia (file, loop)` (audio: the engine's preview; MIDI: `previewLine` with the file's notes, velocity scaled by the level), `stopMediaPreview`, `isMediaPreviewing`, `getMediaPreviewProgress`, `getMediaPreviewSound`, `setMediaPreviewLoop`, `get/setMediaPreviewLevel` (settings `mediaBayLevel`); `beginExport` stops the preview. UI `MediaBayPage` / `MediaBayWindow` (ui/MediaBayPage.h: title + status (`getStatusText`), RESCAN; toolbar search / AUDIO MIDI PRESETS SONGS / rating box / SUBFOLDERS / SHUFFLE / RESET; `Locations` (ListBox: ALL LOCATIONS + each), ADD FOLDER... (chooser), REMOVE; `table` (TableListBox: rating stars (click: `cellClicked`), name with a type colour tab, type, length, tempo, key, format, folder, size, date; `sortOrderChanged`; drag `getDragSourceDescription` = "mediabay:" + paths a line each); previewer (PLAY/STOP, CYCLE, AUTO PLAY, LEVEL, `waveArea` from the preview's peaks, playhead), USE IT; settings `mediaBayTypes`, `mediaBayRating`, `mediaBaySubfolders`, `mediaBayAutoPlay`, `mediaBayCycle`; test API `setSearch`, `setTypeShown`, `setMinimumRating`, `setSubfolders`, `selectLocation`, `resetFilters`, `shuffle`, `sortBy`, `selectRow`, `setRating`, `play`, `stop`, `setAutoPlay`, `setCycle`, `use`, `addLocation`, `getResult`, `getNumResults`). MainView: F5 / command Window.MediaBay / IMPORT item 6 `toggleMediaBay` (settings `mediaBayWindow`), `useMedia` (audio `importAudio` new track at the playhead, MIDI `importMidiFilesNow` new track, preset `TrackActions::addTrackFrom`, song `whenSongCanGo` + `openSong`), a `DragAndDropTarget` for "mediabay:" (ArrangeView isn't interested, so JUCE walks up to MainView) using `fileDragEnter/Move/Exit` and `mediaDropped` (audio/MIDI through `filesDropped`, others `useMedia`).   - **The file browser tree (0.22.1):** MediaDatabase `exclusions` (left-out folders, saved as `Exclude`), `includedBy (file, locations, exclusions)` (the deepest scanned or left-out folder holding it decides), `isIncluded`, `ScanState` none/some/all and `scanState (folder)` (some: something deeper decides otherwise), `setIncluded (folder, scanned)` (what was decided inside it goes; added as a location or exclusion only if the rule doesn't already give it; `dropUnincluded`; the caller rescans), `scanAll` skips files not included; `addLocation` clears an equal exclusion. MediaBayPage `Browser` (a TreeView; hidden open `Root`; `RootItem`s SCANNED FOLDERS / FAVORITES / THIS COMPUTER / USER CONTENT, filled when opened, `refill`; `FolderItem` (box at x < 18, the folder shape, label coloured by state; lazy `load` of non-hidden subfolders; `refreshViews`); `refreshLists` (refills the first two roots, notes and reopens what was open: JUCE's openness state didn't survive the refill), `reveal (folder)` (opens the way from the root holding it), `showFolderMenu`), `selectedFolder` (query.folder), `selectFolder`, `setFolderScanned`, `refreshFolder`, `addFavorite` / `getFavorites` (settings `mediaRackFavorites`, else `localFavorites`), `revealFolder`, `getFolderChildren`; `removeKey` is REFRESH; `locationsWidth` 300; the status line says when the chosen folder isn't scanned; the timer repaints the tree every 10 frames while scanning. MediaRack: `addFavorite` uses `isIncluded`/`setIncluded`; the timer re-reads `mediaRackFavorites` (favorites from the window). **A TreeView lays out its rows on the message loop:** the snapshot runs `runDispatchLoopUntil (60)` first. Manual pages: File Browser Section, Scanning Folders, Rescanning Media Folders / Refresh Views.
  - **0.22.2:** MediaBay `read`: the reader's `metadataValues` (WAV ACID: `WavAudioFormat::acidTempo/Beats/RootSet/RootNote/OneShot`; AIFF Apple loop: `AiffAudioFormat::appleBeats/RootSet/RootNote/Key/OneShot`; tempo from beats / seconds when missing; none for one-shots), `keyFromRootNote (note, mode)`, `info` (bext description, INFO genre/comments/keywords/subject; searched; saved), `readIXml` (our own RIFF walk: JUCE keeps only iXML's ASWG part) + `tagsFromIXml` (`<BWFXML><STEINBERG><ATTR_LIST><ATTR><NAME/><VALUE/>`, names matched by what they hold: category/sub, style/sub, character, comment, tempo/bpm, key; "A | B" takes B) into `MediaItem::fileTags`, moved into `tags` by `takeScanResults` when the file has none of yours. Priority: tempo yours > file > name; key yours > name > file. `MediaItem::readVersion` / `currentReadVersion` 2 (saved as `read`; older items read again by `scanAll`). MediaBayPage: `saveBrowserState` / `loadBrowserState` (settings `mediaBayTreeOpen` (open items' keys a line each: "root:<heading>" or "<heading>|<path>") and `mediaBayFolder`; without settings a static for the run), saved on every opening/closing (`Browser::opennessChanged`, not while `updating`) and `selectFolder`; restored in the constructor (`restoreOpenKeys`, `showSelected`: chosen with dontSendNotification and scrolled to); `isFolderOpenInTree`.   - **0.23.0 (previews in time):** `cmake/Dependencies.cmake` fetches Signalsmith Stretch 1.4.0 (MIT; it fetches signalsmith-linear 0.6.4), linked PRIVATE to PR7SCore; `engine/Stretch.h/.cpp` `stretchAudio (input, rate, outputLength, semitones)` (`exact()`: the whole sound, its latency handled; the header is `signalsmith-stretch/signalsmith-stretch.h`; that one file compiles with warnings off: `-w` / `/W0`). Engine `setPreviewSync (waitForPlay, anchorBeat, lockBeats)` / `isPreviewWaiting`: waiting = silent while stopped (position back to 0); locked = each segment of the block reads at `fmod ((beat - anchor) / lockBeats, 1)` of the sound, going round. Studio `MediaPreviewOptions` (linkTempo, waitForPlay, semitones, toKey, speed; settings `mediaBayLinkTempo`, `mediaBayWaitForPlay`, `mediaBaySemitones`, `mediaBayToKey`, `mediaBaySpeed`), `getMediaPreviewOptions` / `setMediaPreviewOptions`, `planMediaPreview (file, seconds)` (`MediaPreviewPlan`: lengthFactor, semitones, lockBeats, songBpm at the playhead; the file's tempo/beats/key from the MediaBay item, else its name; beats rounded to whole when within 5%; to key the short way), `keyPitchClass`; `previewMedia` stretches audio ahead (up to 90 s), mono made stereo, peaks again; locks from `project.loopStart` when waiting and linked; MIDI previews take semitones and speed. UI: MediaBayPage `linkKey`, `waitKey`, `halfKey`, `doubleKey`, `transposeBox`, `toKeyBox`, `setPreviewOptions` (plays again if playing), the waiting text; MediaRack `linkKey`, `waitKey`, `pitchKey` (`showPitchMenu`), `setPreviewOptions`. Tests: a 440 Hz tone stretched stays 440, +12 reads 880 (zero crossings); the plan; the engine lock read off a ramp. Manual pages: Audio Files Previewer, Previewer Section.

  - **Hitpoints and warping (0.32.0):** `WarpMarker` (beat from the clip's start, seconds into its file), `Part::warp` (sorted; `isWarped` = 2 or more; the edges at 0 and length; XML `Warp`), `Project::HitpointSettings` (threshold 0.25, minimumSeconds 0.06), `hitpointEdits` (per file: added/removed seconds), `audioWarpQuantize` (XML `Hitpoints`). `engine/Warp.h/.cpp`: `detectHitpoints` (spectral flux, 1024/256 Hann, log1p(100|X|), peaks over the 8-frame mean x1.3 + 2% of the loudest, placed where the 32-sample envelope reaches a fifth of the stretch's loudest, kept only if louder after than before: offsets aren't onsets), `filterHitpoints` (threshold, strongest-first minimum length, hand edits within 10 ms), `warpSecondsAt` / `warpBeatAt` (linear in song seconds between markers, own speed outside), `ensureWarp`, `rebaseWarp (old, updated)` (start/length changed, audio kept in place: used by `cutPart`, `updatePart`, `resizePart`, `moveStart`, `moveTakeEdge`), `warpFactors`, `quantizeWarp` (each grid line takes its nearest marker within half a step, in order; the rest in proportion), `WarpRenderer` (per-stretch pieces cached by file/source range/length; own speed copied, else Signalsmith `stretchAudio` with 30 ms before; each stretch after the first also makes 128 samples before its start, faded in over the stretch before: nothing after a marker is stretched with it (no early smear of the next hit)). Engine `WarpSource` (`setWarpSource`): a warped clip plays its rendered sound from offset 0.
